The system of linearised null structure and Bianchi equations is complemented by a set of equation for the linearised frame coefficients. This latter set of equations appears in Section
10.1
and is obtained by combining Propositions
9.6
and
9.7
.

10.1

The equations for the linearised frame coefficients

The equations for the linearised frame coefficients read as follows. We have the transport equations

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}+\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}-\omegah\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle=0\,,

(351)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\nablasl\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}-\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}\,(\eta+\etab)+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\,{}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\yb}\,,

(352)

the mixed transport equations

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\zetao\,,

(353)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}+\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle=\nablasl\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,{}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}+\omegah\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\etao-\zetao

(354)

and the transport equations

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle=-2\,(\eta-\etab,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4})\,,

(355)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\omegah\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\omegabo-2\,(\eta-\etab,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3})-(\eta+\etab,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})\,,

(356)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}-\chi^{\sharp_{1}}\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}+\omegah\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}

\displaystyle=-2\etao+2\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}(\eta-\etab)+2\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}{}^{\sharp}\cdot(\eta-\etab)+(\text{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})(\eta-\etab)\,.

(357)

We have the elliptic equations

\displaystyle\not{\text{curl}}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}+\frac{1}{4}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\text{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})\,,

(358)

\displaystyle\not{\text{curl}}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}-(\eta+\etab)\wedge\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\frac{1}{4}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\text{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})\,.

(359)

10.2

Linearised null structure equations

The linearised null structure equations read as follows. We have the linearised first variational formulae

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}+(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,{}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}

\displaystyle=2\,\chiho+2\,(\etab-\eta)\,\widehat{\otimes}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}\,,

(360)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}+(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\,{}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}

\displaystyle=2\,\chibho+2\,(\not{\mathcal{D}}_{2}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}})-2\,(\etab-\eta)\,\widehat{\otimes}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

(361)

and

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})

\displaystyle=2\,(\trchio)+4\,(\etab-\eta,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4})\,,

(362)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})

\displaystyle=2\,(\trchibo)-4\,(\etab-\eta,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3})-2\,(\not{\textup{div}}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}})-2\,(\textup{tr}\chi)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}-2\,(\textup{tr}\chib)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}\,.

(363)

We have the linearised second variational formulae

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\chiho+(\text{tr}\chi)\chiho-\omegah\,\chiho

\displaystyle=-\alphao\,,

(364)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}\chibho+(\text{tr}\chib)\chibho

\displaystyle=-2\,\not{\mathcal{D}}_{2}^{\star}\ybo+(\etab-\eta)\,\widehat{\otimes}\ybo-\alphabo\,.

(365)

We have the linearised Raychaudhuri equations

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}(\trchio)+(\text{tr}\chi)(\trchio)-\omegah\,(\trchio)=

\displaystyle\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})\,,

(366)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}(\trchibo)+(\text{tr}\chib)(\trchibo)=

\displaystyle\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})+(\text{tr}\chib)\omegabo+2\,\not{\text{div}}\ybo+2\,(\etab-\eta,\ybo)

(367)

\displaystyle-(\nablasl(\text{tr}\chib),\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})-\nablasl_{4}(\text{tr}\chib)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}-\nablasl_{3}(\text{tr}\chib)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}\,.

We have the linearised mixed transport equations

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\chibho+\chi\times\chibho+\omegah\,\chibho=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chib)\chiho+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib){{}^{\star}\chiho}

(368)

\displaystyle-\widehat{(\nablasl\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp_{3}}\cdot\etab}+(\nablasl\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp_{1}}\cdot\etab-\frac{1}{2}\,\etab\,\widehat{\otimes}\,(\nablasl(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}}))-(\not{\textup{div}}\,\etab+(\etab,\etab))\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,({}^{\star}\etab)\,\widehat{\otimes}\,(-2\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})+(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\,({}^{\star}\etab)\,\widehat{\otimes}\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle+(\nablasl_{3}\etab-\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab)\,\widehat{\otimes}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}+(\nablasl_{4}\etab-\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab)\,\widehat{\otimes}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}\chiho+\chib\times\chiho=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)\chibho+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi){{}^{\star}\chibho}-2\,\not{\mathcal{D}}_{2}^{\star}\etao+2\,\eta\,\widehat{\otimes}\,\etao

(369)

\displaystyle-\widehat{(\nablasl\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp_{3}}\cdot\eta}+(\nablasl\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp_{1}}\cdot\eta-\frac{1}{2}\,\eta\,\widehat{\otimes}\,(\nablasl(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}}))-(\not{\textup{div}}\,\eta+(\eta,\eta))\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,({}^{\star}\eta)\,\widehat{\otimes}\,(-2\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})+(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\,({}^{\star}\eta)\,\widehat{\otimes}\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle+(\nablasl_{3}\eta-\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\,\eta)\,\widehat{\otimes}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}+(\nablasl_{4}\eta-\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\eta)\,\widehat{\otimes}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

and

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}(\trchibo)+\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)(\trchibo)+\omegah\,(\trchibo)=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chib)(\trchio)+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})

(370)

\displaystyle-2\,(\not{\textup{div}}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp}\cdot\etab-(\not{\textup{div}}\,\etab+(\etab,\etab))(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})

\displaystyle-(\textup{tr}\chi)\,(-2\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}},\etab)-(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,(-2\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}},{}^{\star}\etab)

\displaystyle+2\,(\textup{tr}\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4},\etab)+2\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4},{}^{\star}\etab)

\displaystyle+2\,(\nablasl_{3}\etab-\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4})+2\,(\nablasl_{4}\etab-\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3})

\displaystyle+(\text{tr}\chi)(\etab,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})+2\rhoo\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}(\trchio)+\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chib)(\trchio)=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)(\trchibo)+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})

(371)

\displaystyle-(\text{tr}\chi)\omegabo+2\,\not{\textup{div}}\etao+4\,(\eta,\etao)

\displaystyle-2\,(\not{\textup{div}}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp}\cdot\eta-(\not{\textup{div}}\,\eta+(\eta,\eta))(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})

\displaystyle-(\textup{tr}\chi)\,(-2\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}},\eta)-(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,(-2\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}},{}^{\star}\eta)

\displaystyle+2\,(\textup{tr}\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4},\eta)+2\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4},{}^{\star}\eta)

\displaystyle+2\,(\nablasl_{3}\eta-\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\eta,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4})+2\,(\nablasl_{4}\eta-\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\eta,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3})

\displaystyle-(\nablasl(\text{tr}\chi),\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})-\nablasl_{4}(\text{tr}\chi)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}-\nablasl_{3}(\text{tr}\chi)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle+(\text{tr}\chi)(\eta,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})+2\rhoo\,.

We have the linearised transport equations for the antitraces

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})+(\text{tr}\chi)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})-\omegah\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})=

\displaystyle\,-(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\trchio)\,,

(372)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})+(\text{tr}\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})=

\displaystyle\,-(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\trchibo)+(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\omegabo-2\,(\eta+\etab)\wedge\ybo+2\,\not{\text{curl}}\,\ybo

(373)

\displaystyle-(\nablasl(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib),\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})-\nablasl_{4}(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}-\nablasl_{3}(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

and the mixed transport equations

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})+\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})+\omegah\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\trchio)-\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\trchibo)

(374)

\displaystyle-2\,(\nablasl_{3}\etab-\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab)\wedge\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}-2\,(\nablasl_{4}\etab-\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab)\wedge\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

\displaystyle+(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\etab,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})-(\not{\textup{curl}}\etab)(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})+2\sigmao\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})+\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chib)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})=

\displaystyle\,-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\trchibo)-\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)(\trchio)

(375)

\displaystyle-(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\omegabo+2\,\not{\textup{curl}}\etao

\displaystyle-2\,(\nablasl_{3}\eta-\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\eta)\wedge\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}-2\,(\nablasl_{4}\eta-\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\eta)\wedge\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}

\displaystyle-(\nablasl(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi),\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})-\nablasl_{4}(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}-\nablasl_{3}(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}

\displaystyle+(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)(\eta,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})-(\not{\textup{curl}}\,\eta)(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})-2\sigmao\,.

We have the linearised transport equations

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\etao+\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)\etao-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi){}^{\star}\etao=

\displaystyle\,\chiho{}^{\sharp}\cdot\etab+\frac{1}{2}\,(\trchio)\etab-\frac{1}{2}\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})({}^{\star}\etab-2\,{}^{\star}\eta)-\betao

(376)

\displaystyle+2\,(\etab-\eta,\eta)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)({}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp_{1}}\cdot(\etab-2\,\eta)\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\ybo+2\,\omegah\ybo=

\displaystyle\,-\chibho{}^{\sharp}\cdot\eta-\frac{1}{2}\,(\trchibo)\,\eta+\frac{1}{2}\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})({}^{\star}\eta-2\,{}^{\star}\etab)-\betabo

(377)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chib)\etao+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)\,{}^{\star}\etao-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib)({}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp_{1}}\cdot(\eta-2\,\etab)

\displaystyle+(\nablasl\etab)^{\sharp_{1}}\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}-(\not{\mathcal{D}}_{2}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}})^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\textup{div}}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}})\,\etab+\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\textup{curl}}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}})\,{}^{\star}\etab

\displaystyle-2\,(\eta-\etab,\etab)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}+(\nablasl_{4}\etab+\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}+(\nablasl_{3}\etab+\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etab)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\omegabo+2\,\omegah\omegabo=

\displaystyle\,-2\,(\eta-\etab,\etao)-2\,(\eta-\etab,\zetao)-2\rhoo

(378)

\displaystyle+((\eta-2\,\etab)\,\widehat{\otimes}\,\eta,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})+(\textup{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})\,(\eta-2\,\etab,\eta)

\displaystyle-(\nablasl\omegah,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}})-(\nablasl_{4}\omegah)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{3}-(\nablasl_{3}\omegah)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}}_{4}\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\zetao+\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\zetao+\omegah\zetao=

\displaystyle\,\chiho{}^{\sharp}\cdot\etab+\frac{1}{2}\,(\trchio)\,\etab+\frac{1}{2}\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})\,{{}^{\star}\etab}-\betao

(379)

\displaystyle+2\,(\etab-\eta,\eta)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}-(\nablasl_{3}\omegah)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}-(\nablasl_{4}\omegah)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)({}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}})^{\sharp_{1}}\cdot\etab\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{3}\zetao+\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\zetao=

\displaystyle\,\nablasl\omegabo+\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\ybo-\omegah\ybo-\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\etao-\betabo

(380)

\displaystyle-\,\chibho{}^{\sharp}\cdot\eta-\frac{1}{2}\,(\trchibo)\,\eta-\frac{1}{2}\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib}){{}^{\star}\eta}

\displaystyle+2\,(\eta-\etab,\eta)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}-(\nablasl_{4}\eta+\chi^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\eta)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}-(\nablasl_{3}\eta+\chib^{\sharp_{2}}\cdot\eta)\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}

Appendix A

Non-integrable frames and the algebraically special frame of Kerr

This section serves as an appendix to the introduction of the paper.

We give an informal definition of non-integrable frames tailored to our problem.

Non-integrable Frames
.

A
non-integrable
null frame
\mathcal{N}
of a Lorentzian manifold
(\mathcal{M},g)
is a null frame whose null frame vector fields
e_{3}
and
e_{4}
induce an orthogonal (relative to
g
) distribution

\mathfrak{D}_{\mathcal{N}}:=\left\langle e_{3},e_{4}\right\rangle^{\perp}

which is a
non-integrable
distribution.
80
80

80

In differential geometry, a
distribution
is a sub-bundle
\mathfrak{D}\subset T\mathcal{M}
of the tangent bundle
T\mathcal{M}
of
\mathcal{M}
. A distribution
\mathfrak{D}
is
integrable
if, for any vector fields
X,Y\in\Gamma(\mathfrak{D})
, one has
[X,Y]\in\Gamma(\mathfrak{D})
.

For the algebraically special frame
\mathcal{N}_{\text{as}}
of the Kerr exterior manifold
(\mathcal{M},g_{a,M})
, one can choose the frame vector fields
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
such that
81
81

81

To give the coordinate form of the frame vector fields
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
, we adopt the standard Boyer–Lindquist differentiable structure. We point out that, although in the paper the spacelike frame vector fields of a null frame are always assumed to be orthonormal, the frame vector fields (
395
) are not orthonormal. This allows a more convenient choice of
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
for the arguments of the present appendix, which can be however (less neatly) repeated for any choice of orthonormal frame vector fields
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
.

\displaystyle e^{\text{as}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{\theta}\,,

\displaystyle e^{\text{as}}_{2}

\displaystyle=|a|\,\sin^{2}\theta\,\partial_{t}+\partial_{\phi}\,.

(395)

The vector field commutator

[e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2}]=|a|\,\sin(2\theta)\,\partial_{t}

(396)

is, for
|a|>0
, such that
[e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2}]\not\in\left\langle e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2}\right\rangle
, and thus
[e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2}]\not\in\Gamma(\mathfrak{D}_{\mathcal{N}_{\text{as}}})
. The distribution
\mathfrak{D}_{\mathcal{N}_{\text{as}}}
is therefore manifestly
non-integrable
for
|a|>0
. For
|a|=0
, the commutator (
396
) identically vanishes and the frame
\mathcal{N}_{\text{as}}
is
integrable
.

The (non-)integrability of null frames has a meaningful relation with the geometry of spacetime foliations. This fact can already be elucidated by considering the non-integrable frame
\mathcal{N}_{\text{as}}
of
(\mathcal{M},g_{a,M})
. By depicting the frame vector fields (
395
) in
(\theta,\phi,t)
coordinates (see Figure
7
), one realises that the vector fields
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
generate
rotating
two-dimensional planes along any line of constant
(\phi,t)
. While it is already evident from (
395
) that the vector fields
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
are not tangent to the two-spheres of constant
(t,r)
, Figure
7
suggests that the distribution of planes generated by
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
cannot be possibly thought (even locally) as the tangent bundle to the two-dimensional leaves of
any
foliation of
\mathcal{M}
.

We remark that the rotating behaviour of the planes generated by
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
, which is crucial to exclude the existence of foliations by two-dimensional leaves whose tangent bundle is (at least locally) generated by
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
, is indeed captured by the failure of the vector field commutator (
396
) to be in the span of
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
. In this sense, the non-integrability of the frame
\mathcal{N}_{\text{as}}
is intimately related to the non-existence of foliations of
\mathcal{M}
with a certain geometry.
82
82

82

One can equivalently think that the non-integrability of the frame
\mathcal{N}_{\text{as}}
excludes the existence of foliations by two-dimensional leaves to which the vector fields
(e^{\text{as}}_{3},e^{\text{as}}_{4})
are (at least locally) orthogonal. A related problem is treated in Appendix B.3 of
[
60
]
, where given a spacetime vector field
X
, one wants to determine whether there exists a family of hypersurfaces foliating the spacetime and (at least locally) orthogonal to the vector field
X
. In this case, the (non-)existence of such hypersurfaces is related to the (non-)integrability of the distribution
\left\langle X\right\rangle^{\perp}
.

\theta
t
\phi
0
\pi/2
p
e_{1}^{\text{as}}
e_{1}^{\text{as}}
e_{2}^{\text{as}}
e_{2}^{\text{as}}

Figure 7
:
The distribution of planes generated by
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
cannot be possibly thought (even locally) as the tangent bundle to the two-dimensional leaves of
any
foliation of
\mathcal{M}
. Indeed, if one assumes that there exists a two-dimensional sub-manifold
\mathcal{S}\subset\mathcal{M}
with
p\in\mathcal{S}
and
T\mathcal{S}=\left\langle e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2}\right\rangle
in an open neighbourhood
\Omega_{p}\subset\mathcal{S}
, then, by the invariance of
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
along the
e^{\text{as}}_{2}
-direction, one can immediately conclude that
\Omega_{p}
has to contain a line segment generated by
e^{\text{as}}_{2}
and passing through
p
. By
\mathcal{S}
being a manifold, the fact that
\Omega_{p}
contains a line segment though
p
implies that
\mathcal{S}
contains an open neighbourhood
\Omega^{\prime}_{p}\subset\left\langle e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2}\right\rangle_{p}
, i.e. a portion of the two-plane generated by
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
at
p
. This latter statement contradicts the fact that the rotation planes generated by
(e^{\text{as}}_{1},e^{\text{as}}_{2})
along the line segment
\left\{\phi=\phi(p),t=t(p)\right\}\cap\Omega^{\prime}_{p}
are tangent to
\mathcal{S}
.

It turns out that Figure
7
is suggestive of a more general fact, which is the manifestation of a classical result by Frobenius:

Frobenius Theorem
.

A null frame
\mathcal{N}
of a Lorentzian manifold
(\mathcal{M},g)
is integrable
if and only if
the frame vector fields
(e_{1},e_{2})
generate, at least locally, the tangent bundle to the two-dimensional leaves of a foliation of
\mathcal{M}
.

The definition and properties of non-integrable frames are rigorously discussed in Section
4.1
.

Appendix B

The hierarchical structure of the system of linearised gravity

We present the hierarchical structure of the system of linearised gravity of Section
10
. The gauge dependent part of the hierarchy only includes outgoing transport equations. All linearised quantities (see Table
2
) appear in the hierarchy of equations.

At each level of the hierarchy, the equations only couple with linearised quantities appearing either higher or at the same level in the hierarchy. The equations which couple at the same level of the hierarchy form pairs of equations of the schematic form

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{q}_{1}

\displaystyle=f\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{q}_{2}+\text{lin.~quantities higher in the hierarchy}\,,

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{q}_{2}

\displaystyle=h\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{q}_{1}+\text{lin.~quantities higher in the hierarchy}

and are denoted by
(\dagger)
. We note that, for all such pairs of coupled equations, at least one of the coupling background quantities
f
and
h
identically vanishes along the event horizon.

The hierarchy reads as follows:

and
\alphabo

The transport equations in the hierarchy have the schematic form

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{f}}}+\frac{p_{1}}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{f}}}+\frac{p_{2}}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,{}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{f}}}+k\,\omegah\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}

\displaystyle=\Gamma\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\Gamma}\,,

(397)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}+\frac{p_{1}}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}+\frac{p_{2}}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,{}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}+k\,\omegah\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}

\displaystyle=\Gamma\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\Gamma}+\not{D}\,\Gamma\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}+\Gamma\cdot\not{D}\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}+\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\psi}\,,

(398)

\displaystyle\nablasl_{4}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}+\frac{p_{1}}{2}\,(\text{tr}\chi)\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}+\frac{p_{2}}{2}\,(\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi)\,{}^{\star}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}+k\,\omegah\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}

\displaystyle=\nablasl\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\psi}+\Gamma\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\psi}+\psi\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\Gamma}+\not{D}\,\psi\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}

(399)

with
p_{1},p_{2},k\in\mathbb{Z}_{0}
, where the quantities
\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{f}}}
,
\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}
denote distinct general linearised induced metric or frame coefficients, the quantities
\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}
,
\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\Gamma}
distinct general linearised connection coefficients, the quantity
\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}
,
\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\psi}
distinct general linearised curvature components and
\not{D}=\left\{\nablasl,\nablasl_{4},\nablasl_{3}\right\}
. The schematic forms (
397
)-(
399
) suppress terms for which the linearised quantity has the same structure of one of the terms already appearing in the equations, but multiplied by lower order derivatives of a background quantity or higher powers of a background quantity. This means, for instance, that the equations (
398
) may contain terms of the form
\Gamma\cdot\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}
or
\Gamma^{2}\cdot\not{D}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\mathfrak{f}}
on the right hand side.

All the equations for the linearised induced metric and frame coefficients do not contain any derivatives of linearised induced metric or frame coefficients on the right hand side. Similarly, all the equations for the linearised connection coefficients do not contain any derivatives of linearised connection coefficients on the right hand side.

The transport equations (
397
)-(
399
) are referred to as
red-shifted
,
no-shifted
or
blue-shifted
if
k
is positive, zero or negative respectively. The equations for the linearised quantities

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{f}}}

\displaystyle=\left\{\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}\,,\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}\right\}\,,

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}

\displaystyle=\left\{\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\underline{\chi}}}\,,\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\text{tr}\underline{\chi}})\,,\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chib})\,,\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\omegah}}\,,\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\xi}}\,,\,\zetao\right\}\,,

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}

\displaystyle=\betabo

are red-shifted, the equations for the linearised quantities

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{f}}}

\displaystyle=\left\{\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\not{g}}}\,,\,(\text{tr}\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{g}})\,,\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\underline{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}\,,\,\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{3}\right\}\,,

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}

\displaystyle=\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\eta}\,,

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}

\displaystyle=\left\{\rhoo\,,\,\sigmao\right\}

are no-shifted and the equations for the linearised quantities

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\mathfrak{f}}}

\displaystyle=\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\mathfrak{f}}}_{4}\,,

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\Gamma}}

\displaystyle=\left\{\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widehat{\chi}}\,,\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\text{tr}\chi})\,,\,(\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\not{\varepsilon}\cdot\chi})\right\}\,,

\displaystyle\overset{\text{\scalebox{.6}{$(1)$}}}{\widetilde{\psi}}

\displaystyle=\betao

are blue-shifted.
